<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">雷以动之，风以散之，雨以润之，日以烜之，艮以止之，兑以说之，乾以君之，坤以藏之。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">雷使万物发动，风使它们散布，雨给它们润泽，太阳把它们晒暖。艮使之止，兑使之悦，乾主其运行，坤收而藏之。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">烜（xuǎn），晒暖、照热。说，在兑卦的语境中读如“悦”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">前四项直接写雷风雨日，后四项换用卦名，八个动词才是贯串全章的线索。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_2dd46cd40579.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-159" name="yz-159"></a>

### 第五章

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">帝出乎震，齐乎巽，相见乎离，致役乎坤，说言乎兑，战乎乾，劳乎坎，成言乎艮。万物出乎震，震东方也。齐乎巽，巽东南也，齐也者，言万物之洁齐也。离也者，明也，万物皆相见，南方之卦也，圣人南面而听天下，向明而治，盖取诸此也。坤也者地也，万物皆致养焉，故曰致役乎坤。兑正秋也，万物之所说也，故曰说；言乎兑。战乎乾，乾西北之卦也，言阴阳相薄也。坎者水也，正北方之卦也，劳卦也，万物之所归也，故曰劳乎坎。艮东北之卦也，万物之所成，终而所成始也，故曰成言乎艮。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">万物的生长以震为出发，震居东方。到东南的巽，万物整齐生长；到南方的离，光明使万物彼此可见，圣人面南听政也取向明之意。坤以地养万物，故说万物在此得到养育。兑应正秋，万物欣悦。乾居西北，阴阳在这里相迫相争。坎是水，居正北，万物劳而归息。艮在东北，事物在这里成就终局，也在这里开启下一轮。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">帝，这里指主宰生化的天帝。致役乎坤，联系下文“致养”读，指地尽其养育之功。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从东方的震走到东北的艮，“终”和“始”在同一处相接，所以方位也可以读成时序。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_720da4487131.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-160" name="yz-160"></a>

### 第六章

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">神也者，妙万物而为言者也。动万物者，莫疾乎雷；桡万物者，莫疾乎风；燥万物者，莫熯乎火；说万物者，莫说乎泽；润万物者，莫润乎水；终万物始万物者，莫盛乎艮。故水火相逮，雷风不相悖，山泽通气，然后能变化，既成万物也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">“神”是就万物变化之微妙而言。要说发动，没有比雷更迅疾的；摇动无过于风，干燥无过于火，悦物无过于泽，润物无过于水。能使万物终而又始，以艮最为显著。水与火能够相及，雷与风彼此不相违逆，山与泽气息相通，由此发生变化而成就万物。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">桡，使弯曲、摇动。熯（hàn），烘干、使干燥。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">前半章分别写各种力量怎样作用，后半章接着问它们怎样相互配合。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_613addc0345e.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-161" name="yz-161"></a>

### 第七章

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乾，健也；坤，顺也；震，动也；巽，入也；坎，陷也；离，丽也；艮，止也；兑，说也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">乾的特性是健行，坤是顺承，震是发动，巽是进入，坎是陷入险中，离是附丽，艮是静止，兑是欣悦。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">丽，这里指依附、附着。说，通“悦”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这八个短小的解释可作读卦时的对照，尤其要留意“离”字在这里取附着之义。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_a6f92252c875.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-162" name="yz-162"></a>

### 第八章

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乾为马，坤为牛，震为龙，巽为鸡，坎为豕，离为雉，艮为狗，兑为羊。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">以动物比配八卦，乾取马，坤取牛，震取龙，巽取鸡，坎取猪，离取野鸡，艮取狗，兑取羊。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">豕（shǐ），猪。雉（zhì），野鸡。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">上一章给每卦一个动词，这一章给出一个动物。两张表可以对读，不必把动物的每个特征都附会上去。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_3929b92c79de.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-163" name="yz-163"></a>

### 第九章

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乾为首，坤为腹，震为足，巽为股，坎为耳，离为目，艮为手，兑为口。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">就人体取象，乾为头，坤为腹，震为脚，巽为大腿，坎为耳，离为眼，艮为手，兑为口。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">股，大腿。首，头。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">看到古文中“股”和“足”并列，便能分清这里所指的两个部位。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_31a53a0dfb70.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-164" name="yz-164"></a>

### 第十章

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乾天也，故称父，坤地也，故称母；震一索而得男，故谓之长男；巽一索而得女，故谓之长女；坎再索而得男，故谓之中男；离再索而得女，故谓之中女；艮三索而得男，故谓之少男；兑三索而得女，故谓之少女。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">乾象天，称父；坤象地，称母。以求得阴阳之爻的位置分列子女：初次所得为震、巽，喻长男、长女；第二次为坎、离，喻中男、中女；第三次为艮、兑，喻少男、少女。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">索，求取。三爻卦的爻位由下向上数，初、中、上三个位置与这里的长、中、少相应。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">把震、坎、艮排在一起看，唯一的阳爻逐卦升高；巽、离、兑的阴爻也有同样的次序。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_1209fa67db6d.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-165" name="yz-165"></a>

### 第十一章

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乾为天、为圜、为君、为父、为玉、为金、为寒、为冰、为大赤、为良马、为老马、为瘠马、为驳马、为木果。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">乾的取象有天、圆、君、父、玉、金，以及寒、冰、深红。说到马，既可取良马、老马，也取瘦马和毛色斑驳的马；说到植物，则有树上的果实。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">坤为地、为母、为布、为釜、为吝啬、为均、为子母牛、为大舆、为文、为众、为柄、其於地也为黑。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">坤的取象有地、母、布帛、锅釜，以及吝啬、均平。它也象征母子牛、大车、纹理、众多和柄。在土色上取黑。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">震为雷、为龙、为玄黄、为敷、为大涂、为长子、为决躁、为苍筤竹、为萑苇。其于马也，为善鸣、为馵足，为作足，为的颡。其于稼也，为反生。其究为健，为蕃鲜。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">震象雷和龙，又取玄黄、铺展、大路、长子、急决、青竹与芦苇。马的善鸣、白足、举足、白额，庄稼的萌生反出，直到刚健、繁盛鲜明，都在本段所举之列。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">巽为木、为风、为长女、为绳直、为工、为白、为长、为高、为进退、为不果、为臭。其於人也，为寡发、为广颡、为多白眼、为近利市三倍。其究为躁卦。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">巽象木与风，又象长女、取直的绳、工匠，兼有白、长、高、进退、迟疑与气味的联想。取象于人，列有发少、额广、眼白多以及趋近利市而获厚利；推至极处又见急躁。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">坎为水、为沟渎、为隐伏、为矫輮、为弓轮。其於人也，为加忧、为心病、为耳痛、为血卦、为赤。其於马也，为美脊、为亟心、为下首、为薄蹄、为曳。其於舆也，为多眚。为通、为月、为盗。其於木也，为坚多心。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">坎象水、沟渠、隐藏、矫直揉曲、弓与轮。在人身上引申到忧虑、心疾、耳痛、血与红色。取象于马，有背美、心急、低头、蹄薄、拖行；取象于车，有多故障。又取贯通、月、盗，以及内里坚实的树木。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">离为火、为日、为电、为中女、为甲胄、为戈兵。其於人也，为大腹，为乾卦。为鳖、为蟹、为蠃、为蚌、为龟。其於木也，为科上槁。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">离象火、太阳、闪电和中女，也取铠甲、兵器、大腹和干燥。鳖、蟹、螺、蚌、龟都有可取之象；在树木则有内空、上部枯槁的形象。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">艮为山、为径路、为小石、为门阙、为果苽、为阍寺、为指、为狗、为鼠、为黔喙之属。其於木也，为坚多节。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">艮象山、小径、石块、门阙、果实瓜类，以及守门人、手指、狗、鼠和黑嘴的动物。树木取坚实而多节者。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">兑为泽、为少女、为巫、为口舌、为毁折、为附决。其於地也，为刚卤。为妾、为羊。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">兑象泽、少女、巫、口舌，也象毁折或附着后脱落。土地取坚硬盐碱者，又有妾和羊的比配。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">“为”在这张表中是取象、比配。坎段的“矫輮”指矫直与揉曲；离段“为乾卦”的“乾”依干燥之义读。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一匹马可以按善鸣归入震，也可按低头归入坎。检索这张象表时，除了问“是什么”，还要问“取它哪一点”。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_2066d5e2cf33.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-g09" name="yz-g09"></a>

## 序卦传

<a id="yz-166" name="yz-166"></a>

### 上篇

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有天地，然后万物生焉。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">盈天地之间者唯万物，故受之以《屯》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《屯》者，盈也；物之始生也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物生必蒙，故受之以《蒙》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《蒙》者，蒙也；物之稚也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物稚不可不养也，故受之以《需》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《需》者，饮食之道也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">饮食必有讼，故受之以《讼》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">讼必有众起，故受之以《师》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《师》者，众也。众必有所比，故受之以《比》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《比》者，比也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">比必有所畜，故受之以《小畜》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物畜然后有礼，故受之以《履》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">履而泰然后安，故受之以《泰》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《泰》者，通也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">先有天地，万物才得以产生。天地之间充满了初生之物，于是乾、坤之后接屯。初生时蒙昧，继以蒙；幼小需要养育，继以与饮食相关的需。饮食分配可能起争讼，讼使众人兴起，便说到师。众人需要亲附，便到比；亲比而后有所积蓄，是小畜。物资积聚，需要礼来安排，便到履。履行得宜，通泰而安，便到泰。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终通，故受之以《否》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终否，故受之以《同人》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">与人同者物必归焉，故受之以《大有》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有大者不可以盈，故受之以《谦》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有大而能谦必豫，故受之以《豫》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">豫必有随，故受之以《随》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">以喜随人者必有事，故受之以《蛊》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《蛊》者，事也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有事而后可大，故受之以《临》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《临》者，大也。物大然后可观，故受之以《观》。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">通畅不能永远持续，转而闭塞，便是否。处境也不能永远闭塞，须与人同心，所以接同人。众人归附，才可能大有。所有虽多，不可自满，故接谦。能谦则豫悦，悦则有人随从。以悦随人而有所事，便要面对蛊所指的事务。任事而能壮大，是临；壮大而可供观看，是观。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">可观而后有所合，故受之以《噬嗑》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">嗑者，合也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可苟合而已，故受之以《贲》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《贲》者，饰也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">致饰然后亨则尽矣，故受之以《剥》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《剥》者，剥也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终尽，剥，穷上反下，故受之以《复》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">复则不妄矣，故受之以《无妄》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有无妄然后可畜，故受之以《大畜》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物畜然后可养，故受之以《颐》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《颐》者，养也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">不养则不可动，故受之以《大过》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终过，故受之以《坎》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《坎》者，陷也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">陷必有所丽，故受之以《离》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《离》者，丽也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">可观而后有所结合，接噬嗑；结合不能草率，还需文饰，接贲。文饰到了尽头会剥落，接剥。剥到上面穷尽，又从下面返回，接复。复归正轨便不妄，接无妄。能不妄才可有更大的蓄积，接大畜。有所蓄积才可供养，接颐。养而后能动，遂接大过。不能一直过越，于是陷入坎；既陷，便须有所依附，终以取附丽之义的离相接。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">受之以，接下来用某卦承接。否在卦名中读 pǐ，指闭塞。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">读到“离”可回看“坎”：这里用身在险中要找依托，解释了为什么上篇以这两卦作结。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_5cd550a89e58.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-167" name="yz-167"></a>

### 下篇

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有天地，然后有万物；有万物，然后有男女；有男女，然后有夫妇；有夫妇，然后有父子；有父子，然后有君臣；有君臣，然后有上下；有上下，然后礼义有所错。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">夫妇之道，不可以不久也，故受之以《恒》。《恒》者，久也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以久居其所，故受之以《遁》。《遁》者，退也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终遁，故受之以《大壮》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终壮，故受之以《晋》。《晋》者，进也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">晋必有所伤，故受之以《明夷》。夷者，伤也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">伤于外者必反其家，故受之以《家人》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">家道穷必乖，故受之以《睽》。《睽》者，乖也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乖必有难，故受之以《蹇》。《蹇》者，难也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终难，故受之以《解》。《解》者，缓也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">从天地、万物说到男女、夫妇，继而说到父子、君臣、上下及礼义的安顿，以此展开下篇。夫妇之道须能长久，由咸而继以恒。事物不能久久停在一地，便有遁退；不能永退，便有大壮；不能永壮，便有晋进。向前可能受伤，接明夷；在外受伤，回到家中，接家人。家道困顿会生乖离，接睽；分歧生难，接蹇；艰难终要舒解，接解。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">缓必有所失，故受之以《损》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">损而不已必益，故受之以《益》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">益而不已必决，故受之以《夬》。《夬》者，决也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">决必有所遇，故受之以《姤》。《姤》者，遇也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物相遇而后聚，故受之以《萃》。《萃》者，聚也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">聚而上者谓之升，故受之以《升》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">升而不已必困，故受之以《困》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">困乎上者必反下，故受之以《井》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">井道不可不革，故受之以《革》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">革物者莫若鼎，故受之以《鼎》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">主器者莫若长子，故受之以《震》。《震》者，动也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">松缓会有损失，接损；损极转益，接益；益到极处须决，接夬；决开之后有所遇，接姤；相遇而聚集，接萃；聚而上行，接升；不断求升终会受困，接困。上方受困便向下求水，所以接井。井也需要除旧更新，接革；能变化生食为熟食的器具，首推鼎，因而接鼎。主掌礼器常由长子担任，长子取象为震，所以继以震。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终动，止之，故受之以《艮》。《艮》者，止也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终止，故受之以《渐》。《渐》者，进也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">进必有所归，故受之以《归妹》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">得其所归者必大，故受之以《丰》。《丰》者，大也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">穷大者必失其所居，故受之以《旅》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">旅而无所容，故受之以《巽》。《巽》者，入也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">入而后说之，故受之以《兑》。《兑》者，说也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">说而后散之，故受之以《涣》。《涣》者，离也。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可以终离，故受之以《节》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">节而信之，故受之以《中孚》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有信者必行之，故受之以《小过》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有过物者必济，故受之以《既济》。</font></p>

<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">物不可穷也，故受之以《未济》终焉。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">动不能不止，便有艮；止也不能永久，须渐次前进，便有渐。进而有所归，接归妹；得到归处而日益丰大，接丰；盛大至极又会失去居所，接旅；羁旅求容，希望能够进入，接巽；进入而相悦，接兑；悦后舒散，接涣。离散不能无穷，需要约束，接节。行事有节使人信服，接中孚；有信而付诸行动，接小过；能越过事物的阻隔，接既济。然而变化不能就此穷尽，全书便以未济收束。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">错，这里作安置讲。“主器”关乎承继、主持祭祀。本页的“遁”与目录的“遯”指同一卦。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">既已渡过，为什么还要另写未济？最后两句把这一串卦名从终点又带回变化之中。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_ecc59b6622a5.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)

<a id="yz-g10" name="yz-g10"></a>

## 杂卦传

<a id="yz-168" name="yz-168"></a>

### 全文

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《乾》刚《坤》柔，《比》乐《师》忧。《临》、《观》之义，或与或求。《屯》见而不失其居，《蒙》杂而著。《震》，起也。《艮》，止也。《损》、《益》盛衰之始也。《大畜》时也。 《无妄》灾也。《萃》聚而《升》不来也。《谦》轻而《豫》怠也。《噬嗑》食也，《贲》无色也。《兑》见而《巽》伏也。《随》无故也，《蛊》则饬也。《剥》烂也，《复》反也。《晋》昼也。《明夷》诛也。《井》通而《困》相遇也。《咸》速也。《恒》久也。《涣》离也。《节》止也。《解》缓也。《蹇》难也。《睽》外也。《家人》内也。《否》、《泰》反其类也。《大壮》则止，《遁》则退也。《大有》众也。《同人》亲也。《革》去故也。《鼎》取新也。《小过》过也。《中孚》信也。《丰》多故也。亲寡《旅》也。《离》上而《坎》下也。《小畜》寡也。《履》不处也。《需》不进也。《讼》不亲也。《大过》颠也。《姤》遇也，柔遇刚也，《渐》女归待男行也。《颐》养正也。《既济》定也。《归妹》女之终也。《未济》男之穷也。《夬》决也，刚决柔也，君子道长，小人道忧也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">乾取刚，坤取柔；比显亲附的喜悦，师显动众的忧虑。临与观可从给予、求取两面来看。屯初生而守住其所，蒙在纷杂中渐有显发。震起，艮止；损益可看作盛衰转换的起点。大畜有待其时，无妄也会遭到意外之灾。萃是聚，升则向上而不返。谦把自己放轻，豫悦容易松懈。噬嗑关乎咬合、食用，贲却说到无色。兑显在外，巽潜入内。随不拘旧，蛊着手整治。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">剥是剥烂，复是返回；晋如白昼，明夷见伤。井能通用，困则有所遭遇。咸感应迅速，恒保持长久。涣离散，节止限，解松缓，蹇艰难；睽相疏于外，家人相亲于内。否与泰的情状相反。大壮须知止，遁则退；大有是所有众多，同人是彼此相亲。革去旧，鼎取新；小过说越过，中孚说诚信。丰盛时事端繁多，旅人则少有亲近者。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">离火上炎，坎水下行。小畜所蓄尚少，履行而不停处；需等待而未进，讼争执而不亲。大过有倾颠之象。姤是相遇，柔与刚相遇；渐用女子出嫁、等待男方依礼而行作比。颐是以正道养育，既济是事情已定。归妹说女子出嫁有归，未济说男子处境尚困。夬是决，一组刚爻决去柔爻，篇末以君子之道增长、小人之道可忧来解释这一卦。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">饬，整顿、修治。故在“随无故”“革去故”中与故旧、既有事物相关。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">《序卦》用前后相承来读卦，这里却让卦义彼此映照。把“革去故”和“鼎取新”放在一处，便能看到同一次变化的两面。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[本则原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_b64fdc9b38d6.aspx)　·　[返回章目](#yz-rest)
